# Extended runtime measurements: SBF v0

Source SHA-256: `86ac10b35f190285bfabf829d3a7782e760c8b6e9d0d487f80a7b524e422c0c4`. See [scope and reproduction](CU.md).

Whole and baseline are separate uninstrumented instructions; difference is whole minus baseline.
Mutable methods include validated load and use the existing trusted mutable idle baseline.
Their differences include validation and fixture assertions, so they are not isolated edit costs.
Read-only ref_methods/to_owned use the existing validated read load baseline. No new phase brackets are reported.
Collections use Max/Prepaid, zero funding CPIs, the default mapping profile and a 20,000,000-CU scaling meter.
Alloc helpers retain the step-40 n=1/2/10 cohort and SDK bump allocator with its default 32 KiB heap.
